      "basis": "stated",
      "direction": "directed",
      "explanation": "The ordinary-pair conclusion supplies a nef model for the absolute rounded-ring argument. Source abundance then makes its rational adjoint semiample.",
      "relation": "uses",
      "source_anchor": "proof-stmt-cor-lc-finite-generation",
      "source_pm_id": "PM-034.4",
      "source_quote": "Suppose now that $D$ is pseudo-effective. The ordinary-pair conclusion of the companion minimal-model theorem (OpenAI 2026a, Corollary 1.2) [[OpenAIMinimalModels2026](#ref-OpenAIMinimalModels2026)] gives a normal projective lc pair $(Y,\\Delta_Y)$ and a birational map $\\phi:X\\dashrightarrow Y$ extracting no divisors, with $\\Delta_Y=\\phi_*\\Delta$ and $D_Y=K_Y+\\Delta_Y$ $\\mathbb{Q}$-Cartier and nef. Log discrepancies do not decrease.",
      "target_anchor": "stmt-cor-ordinary",
      "target_pm_id": "PM-036.3",
      "target_quote": "If $K_X+B$ is pseudo-effective, $K_Y+B_Y$ is nef; otherwise $Y$ admits a Mori fibre contraction with relative Picard number one and relatively ample $-(K_Y+B_Y)$.",
      "verification": "PENDING"
    },
    {
      "basis": "inferred",
      "direction": "directed",
      "explanation": "The log abundance theorem in characteristic zero generalizes the fourfold abundance result by establishing semiampleness of nef log canonical adjoints in all dimensions without dimension bounds.",
      "relation": "generalises",
      "source_anchor": "thm:main",
      "source_pm_id": "PM-034.4",
      "source_quote": "Let $(X,B)$ be a normal projective log canonical pair over an algebraically closed field of characteristic zero.",
      "target_anchor": "stmt-thm-full-abundance",
      "target_pm_id": "PM-034.7",
      "target_quote": "Let $(X,\\Delta)$ be a normal projective log canonical pair of dimension at most four over an algebraically closed field of characteristic zero.",
      "verification": "PENDING"
    },
    {
      "basis": "inferred",
      "direction": "symmetric",
      "explanation": "Both works establish positivity and abundance by restricting the adjoint line bundle to the reduced scheme of the boundary divisor and checking semiampleness there.",
      "relation": "shared_technique",
      "source_anchor": "stmt-prop-boundary-restriction",
      "source_pm_id": "PM-034.4",
      "source_quote": "Then $M|_D$ is semiample as a rational line bundle on the reduced scheme $D$.",
      "target_anchor": "bl:thm:supported",
      "target_pm_id": "PM-034.7",
      "target_quote": "If $\\mathcal O_V(G)|_{G_{\\mathrm{red}}}$ is semiample, then $\\kappa(V,A)>0$.",
      "verification": "PENDING"
    },
    {
      "basis": "inferred",
      "direction": "directed",
      "explanation": "The logarithmic subadditivity formulation incorporates reduced simple normal crossing boundary divisors into fiber spaces, generalising the addition inequality beyond boundary free varieties.",
      "relation": "generalises",
      "source_anchor": "stmt-asm-iitaka",
      "source_pm_id": "PM-034.4",
      "source_quote": "Let $f:X\\to Y$ be a surjective morphism with connected fibers between smooth connected projective complex varieties.",
      "target_anchor": "cor:schnell-general",
      "target_pm_id": "PM-034.8",
      "target_quote": "Let $f\\colon X\\to Y$ be a surjective morphism with connected fibers between smooth connected projective complex varieties",
      "verification": "PENDING"
    },
    {
      "basis": "inferred",
      "direction": "symmetric",
      "explanation": "Both proofs rely on divisonal dlt extraction and boundary restriction to deduce global semiampleness of nef adjoint divisors from lower dimensional boundary components.",
      "relation": "shared_technique",
      "source_anchor": "stmt-prop-boundary-restriction",
      "source_pm_id": "PM-034.4",
      "source_quote": "Let $(V,D)$ be a projective $\\mathbb{Q}$-factorial dlt $n$-fold with $D$ reduced and $M=K_V+D$ nef.",
      "target_anchor": "thm:main",
      "target_pm_id": "PM-034.14",
      "target_quote": "Let $\\Delta$ be an effective rational Weil divisor such that $(X,\\Delta)$ is Kawamata log terminal",
      "verification": "PENDING"
    },
    {
      "basis": "inferred",
      "direction": "symmetric",
      "explanation": "Both works establish good log minimal models for pseudoeffective adjoint divisors by reducing the problem through canonical nonvanishing and inductive fibrations.",
      "relation": "shared_technique",
      "source_anchor": "stmt-prop-inductive-reduction",
      "source_pm_id": "PM-034.4",
      "source_quote": "Then every projective lc $n$-fold with real boundary and pseudo-effective adjoint has a good log minimal model.",
      "target_anchor": "thm:main",
      "target_pm_id": "PM-034.3",
      "target_quote": "Let $X$ be a normal connected compact Kähler fourfold, and let $B$ be an effective rational Weil divisor.",
      "verification": "PENDING"
    },
    {
      "basis": "inferred",
      "direction": "directed",
      "explanation": "Full log abundance in characteristic zero establishes semiampleness unconditionally and across all dimensions without assuming prior nonvanishing.",
      "relation": "generalises",
      "source_anchor": "thm:main",
      "source_pm_id": "PM-034.4",
      "source_quote": "If $K_X+B$ is nef, then some positive Cartier multiple of it is generated by global sections.",
      "target_anchor": "stmt-thm-fourfold",
      "target_pm_id": "PM-034.13",
      "target_quote": "If $\\kappa(X,D)\\ge0$, then $D$ is semiample. If $\\kappa(X,D)=0$, then $D\\sim_{\\mathbb Q}0$.",
